    C r0 r1 r2 r3=1.                                      (7)

The extra quadratic kernel is thus derived on the original full space.

## 2. The declared signed interval family

For 0<=a<b<=4, let J^[a,b] be zero outside rows/columns [a,b].
Its off-diagonal entries inside the interval are

    J_ij=(i-a+1)h_i-1           when i<j,
    J_ij=1+(b-i)h_i             when j<i,                   (8)

and each diagonal is minus its off-diagonal row sum. Put h4=0 solely
as a convention in (8); its value does not affect the final row of a mode.
In particular J e=0 for every mode.

For local indices a<=i<j<=b, put
u_i=(i-a+1)h_i-1 and v_j=1+(b-j)h_j. Multiplication gives
(JT)_ij=u_i v_j and (JT)_ji=-v_j u_i. A column before a vanishes by
the zero row sum; a column after b vanishes by triangularity. Therefore
sym(JT) is diagonal. At i in [a,b] its diagonal is

    (h_i-1)^2(i-a+1)(b-i)-(i-a)(b-i+1).                   (9)

To check (9) without omitting a term, let l=i-a and m=b-i.
The diagonal of JT is
(h_i-1)m((l+1)h_i-1)-l(1+m h_i)
=(h_i-1)^2(l+1)m-l(m+1).

The selected construction is

    N=sum_(a=0)^3 tau_a J^[a,4]
                        +sum_(i=0)^2 q_i J^[i,i+1],       (10)

with ALL seven weights tau_a,q_i real. Define actual Lambda_ij=N_ij
for i!=j and Lambda_ii=0. Since N e=0, its row Laplacian is L=-N.
Require

    N^T e=-omega, sym(NT)=D, Lambda_ij>=0 (i!=j).          (11)

Equations (11) imply M=D-sym(NT)=0 on the entire R5. No smaller
matrix, rank heuristic, numerical factorization or dropped coordinate is
being used. Conversely (11) is the precise zero-remainder family (10).
Signed mode weights are permitted; only the final actual Lambda signs count.

## 3. All seven weights and all twenty entries, explicitly

Assume here h_i>1 for i<4. Define

    S_i=sum_(a=0)^i tau_a, B_i=sum_(j=0)^i S_j, B_(-1)=0,
    U_i=h_i B_i-S_i=r_i B_i+B_(i-1),
    V_i=1+(4-i)h_i,
    K_i=product_(j=0)^i r_j^(-2)-1.                       (12)

B_i here denotes an interval-coordinate cumulative flow, NOT the
project's benchmark B_n. In fact the final outgoing terminal coefficients
are Lambda_4i=S_i, so B_i is their cumulative sum.

The mode formula gives, for every i<j,

    Lambda_ij=U_i+1_(j=i+1)*r_i*q_i,
    Lambda_ji=V_j S_i+1_(j=i+1)*q_i,                      (13)

where q3=0. Let Z_i=(4-i)B_i+q_i. By (9) the diagonal equation at i<4
is r_i^2 Z_i-Z_(i-1)=h_i(2-h_i), with Z_(-1)=0. Since
h_i(2-h_i)=1-r_i^2, this recursion gives Z_i=K_i. The terminal
diagonal is -B3, so it gives B3=C^2-1. Consequently the diagonal
equations are exactly (7) and

    B3=C^2-1, q_i=K_i-(4-i)B_i, i=0,1,2.                (14)

For the cumulative column flow through cut i, put
H_i=sum_(j=i+1)^3(4-j)h_j and E_(i-1)=sum_(j<i)h_j B_j.
Using (13), the backward mass minus forward mass across that cut is

    [2(4-i)+H_i]B_i-(4-i)sum_(j<=i)h_j B_j
                                             +(2-h_i)q_i.

Substitution of (14) reduces this to

    H_i B_i-(4-i)E_(i-1)+(2-h_i)K_i.                     (15)

The required value is 2P_(i+1). Thus the first three cuts uniquely give

    B0=[2h0-(2-h0)K0]/(3h1+2h2+h3),
    B1=[2(h0+h1)+3h0 B0-(2-h1)K1]/(2h2+h3),
    B2=[2(h0+h1+h2)+2(h0 B0+h1 B1)-(2-h2)K2]/h3.         (16)

There is no remaining linear equation hidden in this solution. Indeed,
given (7), (14), (16), recover
S_i=B_i-B_(i-1), tau_i=S_i-S_(i-1), with S_(-1)=0.
All diagonal equations hold by the displayed recursion, and the first
three flow components hold by the first three cuts. If b=N^T e, then
e^T b=0 and
b^T Te=e^T NT e=e^T D e=(-omega)^T Te.
The error b+omega is therefore zero except possibly at indices3,4,
where it has values delta,-delta. Its inner product with Te=e+P is
-h3 delta. Since h3>0, delta=0. All five original flows hold.

For direct use, the twenty entries resulting from (14)--(16) are

    Lambda01=r0(K0-3B0),
    Lambda02=Lambda03=Lambda04=r0 B0,
    Lambda10=K0+3r1 B0,
    Lambda12=r1 K1+B0-2r1 B1,
    Lambda13=Lambda14=r1 B1+B0,
    Lambda20=(1+2h2)B0,
    Lambda21=K1+2r2 B1-(1+2h2)B0,
    Lambda23=r2 K2+B1-r2 B2,
    Lambda24=r2 B2+B1,
    Lambda30=(1+h3)B0,
    Lambda31=(1+h3)(B1-B0),
    Lambda32=K2+r3 B2-(1+h3)B1,
    Lambda34=r3 B3+B2,
    Lambda40=B0, Lambda41=B1-B0,
    Lambda42=B2-B1, Lambda43=B3-B2.                       (17)

This list, its zero diagonal, (7), (14), and (16) specify the entire
original array. Checking the signs in (17) is necessary and sufficient
within (10). In particular, final sign feasibility forces

    0<=B0<=B1<=B2<=B3.                                   (18)

The first expression in (16) simplifies to

    B0=h0(h0^2-2)/[(h0-1)^2(3h1+2h2+h3)].                (19)

Thus every positive feasible member of this particular family has
h0>=sqrt(2). This is NOT a necessary condition for an arbitrary full
primitive certificate; it comes from the seven-mode equations.

## 4. A dense actual calibration below the requested threshold

Let r be the unique positive root of

    r^4(5+4r)=1,

and set h0=h1=h2=h3=t=1+r. The polynomial on the left is strictly
increasing for r>0. At r=3/5 it is 2997/3125<1, while at r=61/100
it is 2575326426/2500000000>1. Hence

    3/5<r<61/100, C=5+4r<186/25<193/25.                 (20)

Define K_i, B_i and all twenty entries by (12), (14), (16), (17).
Then (7) holds exactly, and Section3 proves every flow and M=0.
Here are elementary exact sign checks; no external constant-step theorem
or numerical feasibility is invoked.

Write eta=(1-r)/(1+r). From (20),

    6/25<eta<1/4,
    5/3<K0<16/9, 6<K1<7, 17<K2<21.

For example r^2<3/8 gives the lower K bounds, and r>3/5 gives
the upper bounds. Formula (16) reduces to

    B0=(r^2+2r-1)/(6r^2),
    B1=4/3+B0-eta K1/3,
    B2=6+2(B0+B1)-eta K2.

The derivative of B0 is (1-r)/(3r^3)>0; B0 at 3/5 is 7/27>1/4.
Also r^2+2r-1<3/5 and 6r^2>54/25, giving B0<5/18<3/10.
Consequently

    1/4<B0<3/10,
    1<B1<173/150<6/5,
    13/4<B2<123/25<5,
    B3=C^2-1>1344/25>5.                                 (21)

These bounds prove all four terminal coefficients and every nonadjacent
entry in (17) strictly positive. For the other entries:

    K0-3B0>5/3-9/10>0,
    K1-2B1>6-12/5>0,
    K2-B2>17-5>0,
    Lambda10=K0+3r B0>0,
    Lambda21>6-(211/50)(3/10)>0,
    Lambda32>17-(261/100)(6/5)>0.

The last forward coefficient is Lambda34=r B3+B2>0 as well.
Thus all twenty actual off-diagonal coefficients are strictly positive.
This is a fully specified algebraic dense certificate, with M=0 on R5.
It has capacity below the target, and is not a threshold seed.

## 5. Entire equal-tail family: statement and reductions

Claim. No word h=(u,w,t,t)>=0 with C=1+u+w+2t>=193/25 admits
any certificate of the form (10)--(11), even with arbitrary signed
tau_a,q_i.

By Section1 any putative threshold certificate has all four steps >1.
Write

    a=u-1, b=w-1, d=t-1, a0=sqrt(2)-1,
    C0=193/25, rC=(C-1)/(C+1).

Equations (3), (7), (18), (19) imply

    a0<=a<=rC<1, 0<d<=rC,
    b=C-5-a-2d>0,
    C a b d^2=1.                                        (22)

The proof splits only at d=1/2 and covers both pieces completely.

### 5.1. The whole d>=1/2 piece

On the rectangle a in[a0,rC], d in[1/2,rC], define

    F_C(a,d)=a d^2(C-5-a-2d).

For C>=C0, the last factor is positive throughout this rectangle:
its minimum is C-5-3rC, positive at C0 and increasing thereafter.
For fixed d, F_C is a concave quadratic in a, so its minimum is
at an endpoint of the a interval. At either endpoint a, its derivative
in d is 2a d(C-5-a-3d), which can change sign only from positive
to negative. Hence the minimum in d is also at an endpoint.

The four possible minimum values are

    a0(C-6-a0)/4,
    rC(C-6-rC)/4,
    a0 rC^2(C-5-a0-2rC),
    rC^3(C-5-3rC).                                      (23)

Each is positive and increasing for C>=C0: rC'=2/(C+1)^2,
so each bracket in (23) has positive derivative, and its positive
nonconstant prefactors also increase. At C0 use the rational bounds

    41/100<a0<21/50, 3/4<rC0=84/109<4/5.

They give the following respective lower bounds for (23):

    533/4000, 69/400, 2583/16000, 27/200.

For the third bracket, for example,
C0-5-a0-2rC0>68/25-21/50-8/5=7/10.
For the fourth it is >68/25-12/5=8/25.
Every displayed bound is at least 533/4000. It follows that

    C F_C(a,d) >= (193/25)(533/4000)
                 =102869/100000>1,

contradicting (22). This closes the entire d>=1/2 piece.

### 5.2. The whole 0<d<1/2 piece

Since a<1 and 2d<1,

    b>C-7>=18/25>1/2.                                   (24)

The nonnegative original coefficients Lambda40,Lambda41 give
B1>=B0>=0. The nonnegative original Lambda12 in (17) gives

    b K1+B0-2b B1>=0,

so K1>=(2-1/b)B0>=0. For equal last steps, (16) says

    3t B1=2(u+w)+3u B0-(2-w)K1.

Substitute this expression into the Lambda12 inequality. The result is

    (5+3d-2b)K1 >= 4(u+w)+(6u-3t/b)B0.                  (25)

The last coefficient is positive: u>=sqrt(2)>7/5, b>18/25,
and t<3/2 imply 2ub>252/125>3/2>t. Thus (25) requires

    (5+3d-2b)K1>=4(u+w)>0.                              (26)

In particular both factors on the left are positive. By (22),

    K1=1/(a^2 b^2)-1=C^2 d^4-1<C^2/16-1,
    5+3d-2b<17+7d-2C<41/2-2C,
    u+w=C-3-2d>C-4.

Therefore (26) would imply

    4(C-4)<(41/2-2C)(C^2/16-1).                         (27)

This is impossible for EVERY C>=C0. In fact the left side minus the
right side of (27) is

    g(C)/32, g(C)=4C^3-41C^2+64C+144.

The exact endpoint values are

    g(193/25)=546003/15625>0,
    g'(193/25)=91338/625>0,

and g''(C)=24C-82>0 on C>=C0. Thus g is positive on that whole ray.
This contradicts (27), closing the 0<d<1/2 piece and proving the claim.

Only original coefficient signs, original flow, and the full M=0
identity were used. The proof permits all seven weights to be signed
and every final coefficient to lie on its nonnegative boundary.

## 6. Hostile calibration: the seven-mode family misses a valid ConPP word

The following gives an exact reason not to extend Section5 to all
zero-remainder certificates or to all four-step words with equal tails.

Put A=1+sqrt(2), let b>0 solve b(A+4+b)=4A, and put

    C=A+4+b, kappa=C^2/16,
    h=(sqrt(2),1+b,3/2,3/2).                              (28)

This word has an actual full certificate with M=0. To specify it
explicitly, its only nonzero entries are

    Lambda01=2, Lambda10=2A,
    Lambda14=(kappa-1)b,
    Lambda21=Lambda31=3(kappa-1)/2,
    Lambda23=kappa, Lambda24=2kappa-3/2,
    Lambda32=7kappa/2, Lambda34=10kappa-3/2,
    Lambda41=kappa-1, Lambda42=kappa, Lambda43=14kappa.     (29)

All entries not listed are zero, as is the diagonal. All listed entries
are positive because C>4 and b>0. Here is a full-matrix derivation of
(29) rather than an inference from a smaller principal block.

The one-step sqrt(2) certificate has multipliers2,2A and full remainder
(2-(sqrt(2))^2)(g0-g1)^2=0. The two-step word(3/2,3/2) has

    lambda01=1, lambda02=1/2, lambda10=7/2,
    lambda12=17/2, lambda20=1, lambda21=14.

Its row Laplacian has flow(-3,-3,6), and direct multiplication with
T=[[1,0,0],[3/2,1,0],[3/2,3/2,1]] gives
sym(LT)=-diag(3/4,3/4,-15). Its entire remainder is zero.
Accepted04219554e41d9750 Section11 embeds these two child remainders,
multiplies the right one by kappa, and adds (kappa-1) times the
displayed nonnegative primitive-join interpolation combination. With
bridge index1 and right-child queries2,3,4, this gives exactly (29).
Hence every original flow is correct and the full five-by-five M is zero.

But (28) cannot have a certificate in the seven-mode class (10).
It satisfies (7), because Cb=4A and A(sqrt(2)-1)=1. Equations (16),
(19) force B0=0. Let K1=A^2/b^2-1>0; positivity follows from b<A,
which follows from C>4 and bC=4A. Then (16) simplifies to

    B1=2K1/3.

For explicit verification, its numerator is
(A+b)[b^2+(A+1)b-A]/b^2;
the bracket equals3(A-b) by b^2+(A+4)b=4A, and its denominator
2h2+h3 is9/2. This gives B1=2K1/3 exactly. Formula (17) then forces

    Lambda12=b K1-2b B1=-b K1/3<0.                       (30)

No alternative seven weights can repair (30), since Section3 proved
their uniqueness for fixed positive h. The actual certificate (29)
has Lambda13=0 and Lambda14>0, whereas (17) requires Lambda13=Lambda14.
Releasing that specific repeated forward coefficient is therefore a
necessary change for this calibration, even with arbitrary signed mode
weights and a zero full remainder.

This is a scope counterexample, not a threshold seed: no capacity gain
beyond the original basic composition is claimed for (28). Explicitly,
C is the positive root of C^2-(A+4)C-4A=0. Since A<121/50, this
polynomial at C0=193/25 is greater than89/250>0, proving C<C0.

### 6.1. An explicit full-matrix repair of that calibration

The failed relation can actually be released by a displayed homogeneous
direction, rather than just named as missing. For h=(u,w,z,v) with
b=w-1,c=z-1,d=v-1>0, define a skew-symmetric five-by-five matrix
A_cyc with zero row/column0 and the following upper entries:

    (A_cyc)12=-bcv, (A_cyc)13=b(cd-1), (A_cyc)14=bz,
    (A_cyc)23=-(1+bcd), (A_cyc)24=1-bc, (A_cyc)34=-w.

Let p_tail=(1,-b,bc,-bcd)^T on indices1,2,3,4. Direct multiplication
gives A_cyc e=0 and A_cyc(0,p_tail)^T=0. For example the first of
these four row sums is -bcv+b(cd-1)+bz=0; the corresponding p_tail
row product is b^2 c[v+cd-1-zd]=0. The other three row sums are
bcv-bcd-1+1-bc, -b(cd-1)+bcd+1-w, and -bz-1+bc+w,
respectively, and all vanish. The other three p_tail products are
bc[v-bcd-1-d(1-bc)],
b[1-cd-(1+bcd)+wcd], and
b[-z+1-bc+wc], respectively, and all vanish as well.

For any real rho put Delta L=rho A_cyc T^(-1). Since T^(-1)e=p
from (6) and the last four entries of p are (1-u)p_tail,

    Delta L e=0, (Delta L)^T e=0,
    sym(Delta L T)=0.                                   (31)

Moreover column0 of T^(-1), restricted to indices1,...,4, is
-u p_tail. Thus Delta L has zero row and column0. Its only changed
off-diagonal multipliers, Delta Lambda_ij=-(Delta L)_ij, are

    Delta Lambda12=-rho b v,
    Delta Lambda13= rho b(z+v),
    Delta Lambda14=-rho b z,
    Delta Lambda21= rho c v,
    Delta Lambda23= rho(v+1-bc),
    Delta Lambda24= rho(bc-1),
    Delta Lambda31= rho(1-cd),
    Delta Lambda32= rho[d(1+b+c)-1],
    Delta Lambda34= rho w,
    Delta Lambda41=-rho z,
    Delta Lambda42= rho(w+z),
    Delta Lambda43=-rho w.                              (32)

For an entrywise check, the tail inverse matrix used to obtain (32) is

    [ 1       0       0    0
     -w       1       0    0
      wc     -z       1    0
     -wcd     zd     -v    1 ].

Thus (31)--(32) preserve every original flow and EVERY entry of the
original full M, not just a principal block or one covariance. Actual
nonnegativity must still be checked after the change. This is not an
unconditional sign-preserving move.

At the concrete word (28), put K=kappa-1. The unique seven-mode base is

    B0=0, B1=2K/3,
    B2=1+(20+2b)K/9, B3=16K+15.

These formulas follow from (16) and K(4-b)=2(A+b), which in turn
follows from Cb=4A and kappa=C^2/16. Select rho=-2K/9 in (32).
Then Lambda12 changes from -bK/3 to0, Lambda13 changes from2bK/3
to0, and Lambda14 changes from2bK/3 to bK. The outgoing terminal
entries become K,1+K,14(1+K) at columns1,2,3. For the other changed
entries, substitution in (17) and (32) gives, in order21,23,24,31,32,34,

    3K/2, 1+K, 1/2+2K, 3K/2, (7/2)(1+K), 17/2+10K.

Together with the unchanged entries these are exactly (29), so every
sign is now nonnegative. This is a constructive repair of the actual
negative coefficient, with full-space PSD preserved identically.
It demonstrates an essential non-tree skew freedom absent from (10).
It supplies no threshold word: the repaired word still has C<C0.

## 7. What is closed, and what remains to do

Closed at joint-draft level: the entire declared seven-mode construction
on every nonnegative equal-tail word(u,w,t,t) with sum>=168/25. The
calibration in Section4 has all twenty entries positive and full M=0,
so the family is an actual dense certificate mechanism. Section6 proves
its loss of freedom by one explicit negative ORIGINAL multiplier at
a valid full ConPP word, and (31)--(32) repair that entire actual array.

Still open: the broader seven-mode family with unequal tails, the full
ten-interval zero-remainder family, every general nonzero PSD remainder,
the original threshold cone, and the original all-word exact-profile Goal.
The evidence does not show that a survivor of every old necessary test
exists, and supplies no such claimed survivor.

In particular, repeating the variable-step interval identity or changing
only the seven weights in the excluded equal-tail family cannot produce
the requested seed. A meaningful continuation must release an actual
coefficient relation, such as Lambda13=Lambda14, or the zero-remainder
restriction, and must again provide all twenty signs and full-space PSD.
The direction (32) releases that relation and is fully calibrated, but
its simultaneous signs at an actual threshold word remain unmatched.
No generic unresolved feasibility formulation is claimed as a solution.

One allocated analytical round remains after this round returns. No
numerical probe is proposed here: the two actual calibration objects and
the entire selected obstruction have exact symbolic decisions. No formal
gate, resource renewal, truth-store edit or process action is authorized
or implied by this handoff.

# Free-word positive-factor certificates cannot exceed basic capacity

Status: complete joint proof DRAFT, UNVERIFIED. This is max2 round37,
the FIRST of two analytical rounds allocated after completed36 by
TASK.md and master_guidance3acdda5439874057. The completed ceiling is38,
and attempted ceiling39 includes one historical interruption. No numerical
evaluation, solver, machine algebra, replay, formal-verifier call,
installation, consult, or subordinate agent was used. One assigned round
remains after this handoff. The original Goal remains OPEN.

This draft studies ALL positive step excesses in the two-sequence
factorization. No constant, alternating, palindromic, monotone, or other
word pattern is imposed. At length5 there are four independent positive
excesses and a final balance root; at length6 there are five and a final
balance root. All30 or42 original ordered multipliers and all6 or7
independent formal gradients are retained. The restriction to positive
factors and a zero original matrix remainder is declared throughout.

Accepted inputs are fact04219554e41d9750, especially its proof Sections2,
10--13, for the benchmark scalar recurrence, original full certificate,
physical joins, and arbitrary-length physical substitution; and
fact1252b8823bb5a290 for the original smooth interpolation convention.
The earlier draft0421d0c1066b8f2d is recalled as awareness only. Its
variable-step formulas and every new ingredient needed here are proved
jointly below. There is no use of an unverified result as a premise.

The conclusion is a scope-complete obstruction of the enlarged declared
class: its capacity A is at most C_r at every length r, and is strictly
less than C_r for r=5 and6. Thus no member reaches A>Z_6/Z_1 or A>Z_7/Z_1.
In fact no fixed integer ray can rescue one: Z_((r+1)b)>=C_r Z_b for
every b>=1. Its actual primitive-leaf substitution has normalized error
strictly ABOVE the original benchmark at every nonempty input-tree size
when r=5 or6. This is not a conclusion about the unrestricted primitive
cone, other factorizations, nonzero PSD remainders, or unrestricted W.

The new bridge is an interval-product constraint forced by the factors.
A separate, larger scalar relaxation with all these interval constraints
has exact maximum C_r. The proof uses tight intervals and a perturbation
of two unconstrained coordinates; it is not a numerical optimization.

All newly introduced local symbols below have the implicit suffix fw37.
In particular U_i,D_i are factor sequences, not the benchmark U_n^ZJ;
P_k,R_k are prefix quantities; and q_I is an interval product.

## 1. The original full-space certificate and the declared factor class

Let r>=1, let h_i=1+t_i with t_i>0 for 0<=i<r, and put

 s=sum_(i<r)h_i,       A=1+s.

All r+1 formal gradients g_0,...,g_r and values f_0,...,f_r remain
independent. At normalized smoothness1 set

 x_i=x_0-sum_(j<i)h_j g_j,
 Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2,
 V_h=2sum_(i<r)h_i(f_i-f_r)
       +sum_(i<r)h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2-sA||g_r||^2.

The full certificate uses Lambda_ij>=0 for every i!=j and Lambda_ii=0.
Let e be the all-ones vector of length r+1, and define

 T_ii=1, T_it=h_t for t<i, T_it=0 for t>i,
 L_flow=diag(Lambda e)-Lambda,
 D_prim=diag(h_i(2-h_i) for i<r, -s(s+2)).

The required flow and matrix are

 L_flow^T e=(-2h_0,...,-2h_(r-1),2s)^T,
 M_prim=D_prim+(L_flow T+T^T L_flow^T)/2.                 (1.1)

Accepted04219554e41d9750 gives the polynomial identity

 V_h+sum_(i!=j)Lambda_ij Q_ij
                  =<M_prim,Gram(g_0,...,g_r)>.          (1.2)

For this candidate class only, require M_prim=0 on the entire original
space. Write Qbar_ij=-2Q_ji, so explicitly

 Qbar_ij=2(f_i-f_j)-2<g_j,x_i-x_j>-||g_i-g_j||^2.

Choose finite strictly positive factors U_0,...,U_(r-1),D_1,...,D_r.
Set U_-1=0,D_(r+1)=0, and set t_r=0 only as an auxiliary placeholder.
For every 0<=i<j<=r define

 mu_ij=(U_i-U_(i-1))(D_j+t_j D_(j+1)),
 mu_ji=(U_(i-1)+t_i U_i)(D_j-D_(j+1)),
 Lambda_ij=2mu_ji,   Lambda_ji=2mu_ij.                  (1.3)

There is no unused ordered entry. The required orientation is
Lambda=2mu^T, because Qbar_ij=-2Q_ji. The declared class consists of
ALL these positive-factor choices with nonnegative original entries
and the exact original flow and M_prim=0. Equivalently,

                     V_h=sum_(i!=j)mu_ij Qbar_ij.        (1.4)

From mu_(i,r)=(U_i-U_(i-1))D_r and
mu_(j,0)=t_0 U_0(D_j-D_(j+1)), nonnegative entries force

 U_i>=U_(i-1),          D_j>=D_(j+1).                    (1.5)

Conversely these monotonicities make every entry in(1.3) nonnegative,
since all t_i,U_i,D_j in their stated ranges are positive. Equality in
(1.5), hence zero original entries, is allowed. A simultaneous scaling
U_i -> c U_i, D_j -> D_j/c with c>0 does not change any multiplier.

## 2. Complete coefficient calculations

For i<j, telescoping gives

 sum_(a<=i)mu_(a,j)=U_i(D_j+t_j D_(j+1)),
 sum_(a>j)mu_(a,i)=(U_(i-1)+t_i U_i)D_(j+1).

The coefficient of <g_i,g_j> in sum mu Qbar is

 2(mu_ij+mu_ji)
 -2h_i U_i(D_j+t_j D_(j+1))
 +2h_j(U_(i-1)+t_i U_i)D_(j+1).                         (2.1)

At j=r the last term is zero; h_r=1 may be used as an auxiliary
placeholder there. Direct expansion of(1.3) gives

 mu_ij+mu_ji=h_i U_i(D_j+t_j D_(j+1))
               -h_j(U_(i-1)+t_i U_i)D_(j+1),

so(2.1) vanishes for every pair and for arbitrary unequal steps.

Put d_i=sum_(j!=i)(mu_ij-mu_ji), p_0=0, and
p_k=U_(k-1)D_k for 1<=k<=r. The function coefficients in(1.4) give

 d_i=h_i for i<r,       d_r=-s.                          (2.2)

At a nonterminal i the incoming weights from smaller and larger
indices respectively are

 I_i=U_(i-1)(D_i+t_iD_(i+1)),
 J_i=(U_(i-1)+t_iU_i)D_(i+1),

with I_0=0. Hence t_i J_i-I_i=t_i^2 p_(i+1)-p_i. The coefficient
of ||g_i||^2 equals

 -d_i-2I_i-2J_i+2h_iJ_i
             =-h_i+2(t_i^2p_(i+1)-p_i).

Matching it with -h_i t_i from V_h proves

 t_i^2(2p_(i+1)+1)=2p_i+1.                              (2.3)

The terminal incoming weight is p_r, and its coefficient is s-2p_r.
Matching -sA gives 2p_r+1=A^2.

Define prefix capacity and reciprocal product by

 P_k=1+sum_(i<k)(1+t_i),
 R_k=product_(i<k)t_i^(-1),       P_0=R_0=1.

Equations(2.3) and the terminal coefficient now force

 p_k=(R_k^2-1)/2>0 for 1<=k<=r,
 R_r=P_r=A,               A product_(i<r)t_i=1.         (2.4)

In particular R_k>1 follows from positivity of the factors, not from
an assumption that each t_i<1. Individual long steps are permitted.

## 3. Necessity of the prefix formulas for EVERY positive representation

It is necessary to show that the familiar factor recurrence covers
the entire declared class, rather than only one convenient construction.
For 0<=k<r put

 u_k=(sum_(i<k)h_iU_i)/U_k,
 v_k=(sum_(j=k+2)^r h_(j-1)D_j)/D_(k+1).

The cut flow telescopes, using(1.3), to

 sum_(i<=k)d_i=p_(k+1)(1-t_k+v_k-u_k)=P_(k+1)-1.        (3.1)

Indeed the outgoing cut sum is p_(k+1)(1+v_k), while its incoming
cut sum is p_(k+1)(t_k+u_k). Also u_0=0 and v_(r-1)=0.
All v_k with k<r-1 are strictly positive.

We prove the exact formulas

 u_k=(P_k-R_k)/(1+R_(k+1)),
 v_k=(P_(k+1)-R_(k+1))/(R_(k+1)-1).                     (3.2)

At k=0 the first is u_0=0. Substituting p_1=(R_1^2-1)/2 and
t_0=1/R_1 into(3.1) gives the second. Its positive value when r>=2
already implies P_1>R_1.

For the induction step let 1<=k<r and abbreviate

 P=P_k, R=R_k, Y=R_(k+1), q=U_k/U_(k-1)>0.

The preceding v formula and positivity give P>R>1. The recurrences
and the product p_k=U_(k-1)D_k give

 u_k=(u_(k-1)+h_(k-1))/q,
 v_(k-1)=[p_(k+1)/(p_k q)](h_k+v_k).

The induction hypothesis supplies

 u_(k-1)+h_(k-1)=(P+R)/(1+R),
 v_(k-1)=(P-R)/(R-1).

By(3.1), v_k=u_k+delta_k with

 delta_k=2(P+t_k)/(Y^2-1)-1+t_k,
 p_(k+1)(h_k+delta_k)=P+RY.

Eliminating u_k and v_k gives the following quadratic for q:

 (1+R)(P-R)q^2-2(P+RY)q
                   -(Y^2-1)(P+R)/(1+R)=0.              (3.3)

Its two roots are exactly

 (P+R)(1+Y)/[(1+R)(P-R)],        (1-Y)/(1+R).

This follows by using
(P+RY)^2+(P^2-R^2)(Y^2-1)=(PY+R)^2
in the quadratic formula. The second root is negative, whereas q>0.
Thus the first is the only possible ratio. Inserting it gives
u_k=(P-R)/(1+Y). Adding delta_k gives the v formula, since

 (P-t_kY)(Y-1)+2(P+t_k)+(t_k-1)(Y^2-1)
                        =(Y+1)(P+1+t_k-Y).

This finishes the induction. Consequently every representation obeys

 1<R_k<P_k for 1<=k<r,
 U_k/U_(k-1)=(P_k+R_k)(1+R_(k+1))
                /[(1+R_k)(P_k-R_k)],                  (3.4)
 D_(k+1)/D_k=(P_k-R_k)(R_(k+1)-1)
                /[(R_k-1)(P_k+R_k)].                  (3.5)

The strict prefix inequalities follow from v_(k-1)>0. No uniqueness
of the full unrestricted primitive certificate has been claimed; only
the ratios inside the explicitly declared two-sequence family are forced.

## 4. Exact free-word construction and all sign conditions

For completeness, the converse also holds. Take arbitrary positive
excesses t_0,...,t_(r-1) satisfying R_r=P_r and 1<R_k<P_k at every
proper nonempty prefix. Normalize U_0=1, define U by(3.4), and set
D_k=(R_k^2-1)/(2U_(k-1)). Formula(3.5) follows by division.

For each 1<=k<r, all factor signs are nonnegative exactly when

 t_k >= (P_k-R_k)/(P_k+R_k-2),
 t_k(P_k-R_k-2) <= P_k+R_k.                              (4.1)

The denominator of the first bound is positive. Inserting
R_(k+1)=R_k/t_k into U_k/U_(k-1)>=1 and D_(k+1)/D_k<=1 proves
these two equivalences by multiplication by positive denominators.
The second inequality is left in the displayed form: division by
P_k-R_k-2, whose sign may change, would be incorrect without a case split.

To check the full identity in this converse, the u formula(3.2) follows
forward from its recurrence and(3.4). The v formula follows backward
from v_(r-1)=0 and(3.5):

 h_k+v_k=(P_k+R_k)/(R_(k+1)-1),
 [D_(k+1)/D_k](h_k+v_k)=(P_k-R_k)/(R_k-1).

For each k set R=R_(k+1),P=P_k,t=t_k. Then

 p_(k+1)(v_k-u_k)
  =[(R+1)(P+1+t-R)-(R-1)(P-tR)]/2
  =P+t+(t-1)(R^2-1)/2.

Adding p_(k+1)(1-t) proves the cut flow(3.1). Differences give
d_i=h_i and total flow zero gives d_r=-s. Equation(2.3) proves every
diagonal match, and Section2 proves every mixed match. Thus(1.4)
holds on ALL original formal coordinates, the original array is
Lambda=2mu^T>=0, its flow is(1.1), and M_prim=0 identically.

There is no uncomputed PSD test. There are only the explicit scalar
sign conditions on this particular exact factorization. Strict U and D
monotonicities make every original off-diagonal entry strictly positive;
weak monotonicities also remain in the class studied below.

For the initially proposed genuinely free parameterization, choose
t_0,...,t_(r-2)>0 independently. Put P=P_(r-1), R=R_(r-1), and let
the final excess be the unique positive root

              t^2+(P+1)t-R=0.                            (4.2)

This is exactly R/t=P+1+t, so it imposes R_r=P_r and nothing about
any equality or pattern among earlier steps. The remaining prefix and
sign tests are precisely(3.4),(4.1). This covers all the declared class,
up to the harmless positive gauge of its factors, by Section3.

## 5. The new consequence: every contiguous block is product-admissible

For each nonempty contiguous interval I=[l,k) of physical indices,
define its mass, capacity, and product by

 H_I=sum_(i=l)^(k-1)h_i,
 K_I=1+H_I,
 q_I=product_(i=l)^(k-1)t_i.

We prove

                     K_I q_I>=1.                        (5.1)

Equality holds for the whole word. If k<r, the inequality is strict.
This is a consequence of the declared factor class, not a necessary
condition asserted for every primitive word.

For l=0, q_I=1/R_k, and(5.1) is P_k>=R_k, already proved.
Now assume 1<=l<k<=r, and write

 P=P_l>1, R=R_l>1, H=H_I>0, q=q_I,
 P_k=P+H, R_k=R/q,
 b_j=(P_j-R_j)/(R_j-1) for 1<=j<=r.

Formula(3.2) identifies b_j=v_(j-1), including b_r=0. The weighted
tail sum defining v_(l-1) decomposes exactly as

 b_l=sum_(i=l)^(k-1) h_i D_(i+1)/D_l +(D_k/D_l)b_k.

By D monotonicity and b_k>=0 this gives b_l<=H+b_k. Since
b_l+1=(P-1)/(R-1) and b_k+1=q(P+H-1)/(R-q), both denominators
are positive. Multiplication and cancellation yield

                 q>=1-H(R-1)/(P-1).                     (5.2)

The other endpoint condition R_k<=P_k gives R<=q(P+H). Combining
this with(5.2), whose right side decreases in R, yields

 q>=1-H[q(P+H)-1]/(P-1),
 q[P-1+H(P+H)]>=P-1+H.

Here P-1+H(P+H)=(H+1)(P-1+H). Dividing by P-1+H>0 proves
q>=1/(1+H), which is(5.1). If k<r, then R_k<P_k, so the second
inequality is strict and(5.1) is strict as claimed.

For a suffix [l,r), equality in(5.1) holds exactly when
D_l=D_(l+1)=...=D_r. Indeed the terminal prefix equality is exact,
and equality in the tail estimate with b_r=0 requires every positive
summand h_i D_(i+1)/D_l to have D_(i+1)=D_l. This also checks the
possibility of zero multipliers on the boundary of the factor class.

## 6. Exact extremal theorem for the larger hereditary scalar class

This section is a separate elementary proof, included here in full.
For any r>=1, call a positive vector t=(t_0,...,t_(r-1)) admissible
if, with h_i=1+t_i, it satisfies

 K_I q_I>=1 for EVERY nonempty contiguous I,
 A product_(i<r)t_i=1,       A=1+sum_(i<r)(1+t_i).        (6.1)

The empty word has capacity1. Define the benchmark kernel and recurrence
as in accepted04219554e41d9750:

 Omega(a,c)=(a+c+sqrt((a+c)^2+4ac))/2,
 C_0=1,
 C_r=max_(0<=j<r)Omega(C_j,C_(r-j-1)).                   (6.2)

We prove that the maximum capacity over(6.1) is exactly C_r. Moreover
every capacity maximizer admits a recursive maximizing split at a single
bridge index. This describes maximizers of(6.1), not every admissible
vector and not every primitive certificate.

First, maxima exist whenever the admissible set is nonempty. A singleton
condition is t_i(2+t_i)>=1, hence t_i>=c_*=sqrt(2)-1>0. Global equality
implies

 1=A product_i t_i >= A t_j c_*^(r-1) >= t_j c_*^(r-1).

Thus c_*<=t_j<=c_*^(1-r). The conditions are closed on this compact
box. The capacity is continuous, so it attains a maximum.

Fix such a maximizing vector. An interval is called tight when K_I q_I=1.
For notational convenience set K_empty=q_empty=1. For each interval put

 F_I=log(K_I)+sum_(i in I)log(t_i),

so F_I>=0, and tightness is F_I=0. Two tight intervals cannot overlap
without one containing the other. Indeed, if their consecutive left-only,
common, and right-only pieces have physical masses a>0,b>0,c>0, then

 F_I+F_J-F_(I union J)-F_(I intersection J)
 =log[(1+a+b)(1+b+c)/((1+b)(1+a+b+c))]>0,                (6.3)

because the numerator minus denominator inside the logarithm is ac>0.
If I,J were both tight, their left side would be nonpositive by(6.1),
a contradiction. Likewise two adjacent disjoint nonempty tight intervals
of masses a,c cannot occur: their union is an interval, and

 F_I+F_J-F_(I union J)=log[(1+a)(1+c)/(1+a+c)]>0.

Consider now the inclusion-maximal PROPER tight intervals. The first
observation makes them disjoint; the second says any two are separated
by at least one index outside them. Call an index uncovered when it is
in none of these maximal proper intervals, equivalently in no proper
tight interval at all.

There must be at least one uncovered index. Otherwise the proper maximal
intervals would cover the word. A single one cannot cover it because it
is proper, and multiple disjoint ones would then have an adjacent pair,
which was just ruled out.

There cannot be two uncovered indices u,v. To prove this, perturb only
t_u,t_v by t_u -> t_u+epsilon, t_v -> t_v-epsilon. Choose a sufficiently
small nonzero epsilon with epsilon(t_v-t_u)<=0. Positivity persists,
the total sum and A remain unchanged, every proper tight interval is
unchanged, and all other proper interval inequalities remain strict by
continuity and finiteness. But

 (t_u+epsilon)(t_v-epsilon)
     =t_u t_v+epsilon(t_v-t_u)-epsilon^2<t_u t_v.

The global product condition therefore becomes strictly less than1.
Starting at this perturbed vector, increase t_u alone by a positive
amount delta until global equality is restored. Such a finite delta
exists: the global product times A is a strictly increasing continuous
function of t_u and tends to infinity as t_u tends to infinity. Every
proper interval expression is nondecreasing under this increase, so all
its inequalities remain valid. The resulting admissible vector has
capacity A+delta>A, contradicting maximality.

There is therefore exactly ONE uncovered index, say j. All indices to
its left, if any, constitute a single maximal proper tight interval,
because multiple maximal intervals would need another uncovered index
between them. The same holds on its right. Consequently the left word
[0,j) and the right word [j+1,r) are each either empty or saturated
admissible words. Let their capacities be a,c and let d=t_j>0. Their
products are 1/a,1/c. The total capacity and saturation read

                  A=a+c+d,       A d=ac.                (6.4)

Thus A=Omega(a,c). Induction on r gives a<=C_j and c<=C_(r-j-1).
The kernel is increasing in both arguments, so

                  A<=Omega(C_j,C_(r-j-1))<=C_r.         (6.5)

This proves the upper bound for every admissible vector, because A was
a global maximizer. In an equality case strict monotonicity of Omega
for positive arguments forces the two children also to be maximizers,
which proves the asserted recursive structure of maximizers.

For completeness the bound is attained within the scalar class. Join
two saturated admissible child words of capacities a,c by h_bridge=1+d,
where d>0 solves d(a+c+d)=ac. An interval inside a child is admissible.
For an interval crossing the bridge, its left suffix and right prefix
have capacities x<=a,y<=c, where an empty portion has capacity1.
Its product is at least d/(xy), and its capacity is x+y+d. Because

 xy/(x+y+d) <= ac/(a+c+d)=d,

the interval is admissible. The displayed monotonicity follows directly
from the partial derivatives y(y+d)/(x+y+d)^2 and x(x+d)/(x+y+d)^2,
which are positive. Full saturation follows from d/(ac)=1/(a+c+d).
Starting from empty children and using a maximizing split in(6.2) now
constructs a saturated admissible word of capacity C_r. These are also
the actual original primitive compositions supplied by the accepted
certificate join. No auxiliary continuous phase or different recurrence
has replaced the original all-split C recurrence.

## 7. Strict capacity loss for the free positive-factor class at r=5,6

Sections5--6 already prove A<=C_r for every length. To obtain strictness
at the two assigned lengths, suppose a positive-factor member had A=C_r.
It would maximize the scalar class(6.1), so Section6 supplies its unique
uncovered bridge index j. But every proper prefix is strictly admissible
by Section5. The left tight child [0,j) must therefore be empty, forcing
j=0. Its capacity would satisfy

                        A<=Omega(1,C_(r-1)).             (7.1)

We now give exact inequalities showing this is smaller than C_r for
r=5,6. Let lambda=1+sqrt(2) and x=C_2. The balanced primitive scalar
identities in accepted04219554e41d9750, proof Section2, give

 C_3=lambda^2, C_4=Omega(lambda,x),
 C_5=lambda x, C_6=Omega(x,lambda^2),
 x^2-(1+lambda)x-lambda=0.

The rational bounds

                 12/5<lambda<17/7,       4<x<81/20      (7.2)

follow as follows. The square-root comparisons use (7/5)^2<2<(10/7)^2.
At x=4 the polynomial is 12-5lambda<0. At x=81/20 and with lambda
replaced by its upper bound17/7, its value is247/2800>0. The polynomial
decreases as lambda increases, so these comparisons locate its positive root.

For C_4, at X=31/4 the defining polynomial for Omega(17/7,81/20) has
value

 X^2-(17/7+81/20)X-(17/7)(81/20)=1/56>0.

Thus C_4<31/4. Meanwhile C_5>48/5. The increasing function
g(X)=X(X-1)/(X+1), for X>sqrt(2)-1, satisfies

 g(48/5)=2064/265>31/4,

where the last cross-multiplied difference is41>0. As
Omega(1,c)<X is equivalent to c<g(X) for these positive quantities,
we obtain Omega(1,C_4)<C_5.

For C_6, (7.2) gives x>4 and lambda^2>29/5. At X=47/4 the polynomial
for Omega(4,29/5) is -23/80<0, so C_6>47/4. Also C_5<1377/140.
Now

 g(47/4)=2021/204>1377/140,

with cross-multiplied difference2032>0. Hence Omega(1,C_5)<C_6.
These two strict comparisons contradict(7.1), proving

             A<C_5 when r=5,    A<C_6 when r=6.          (7.3)

No assertion of a uniform positive gap over the whole factor family is
made. The proof applies to all finite positive factors, including cases
where some successive U or D values coincide. Allowing arbitrary word
patterns has not removed the capacity obstruction.

## 8. Correct true-ray and actual physical-horizon comparisons

Set m=r+1. The fixed requested rays remain b=1, but the obstruction
holds on every fixed positive integer ray b. Specifically,

                         Z_(mb)>=C_r Z_b.                (8.1)

Here Z_b is the actual positive finite ray limit from accepted fact
04219554e41d9750; this is a LOWER comparison, not an upper enclosure.
To prove it from the original recurrences, expand a maximizing B_n
tree, replace every primitive empty leaf by a maximizing primitive
tree of length r and capacity C_r, and replace its single dominant
empty leaf by a maximizing dominant tree of length r and mass B_r.
The original recurrences give B_r>=C_r, as shown in proof Section3
of that accepted fact. There are n+1 leaves in the original tree;
the resulting original tree has m(n+1)-1 steps. Homogeneity and
monotonicity of its Omega and G joins give

                   B_(m(n+1)-1)>=C_r B_n.

Taking n=b*2^j-1, dividing by lambda^j, and passing to the accepted
ray limits proves(8.1). Thus every member at r=5 or6 satisfies

 Z_6/(A Z_1)>=C_5/A>1,
 Z_7/(A Z_1)>=C_6/A>1,                                  (8.2)

respectively. More generally Z_(mb)/(A Z_b)>=C_r/A>1 for every b>=1.
Neither a different integer ray nor a sharper sufficient upper threshold
can turn such a seed into the required strict improvement.

The actual physical substitution is also explicit. Take a fixed fully
expanded maximizing B_n tree. Replace only its n primitive empty leaves
by a seed from the declared factor class, leaving its one dominant empty
leaf physically empty, and recompute every physical bridge by the accepted
joins. Denote the resulting word H_A and its mass D_A=1+2sum H_A.
The accepted arbitrary-length substitution in proof Section12 gives

 length(H_A)=mn,    W_(mn)(H_A)=1/D_A,
 (sqrt(A B_n)-(sqrt(A)-1))^2<=D_A<=A B_n.                (8.3)

Here equality for W follows from the full all-function certificate and
the accepted actual Huber attaining example; it is not a claim restricted
to a test family. In particular the original W=2R normalization is used.

Repeat the same substitution using a maximizing basic primitive seed of
the same length r and capacity C=C_r. The resulting actual word is an
original basic dominant tree of length mn with mass D_C. Therefore
B_(mn)>=D_C. When r=5 or6, C>A, and strict monotonicity of the joins
gives D_C>D_A for every n>=1. Consequently

               W_(mn)(H_A)>U_(mn)^ZJ,  every n>=1.      (8.4)

The unchanged dominant leaf is paid for in the more quantitative bound

 B_(mn)W_(mn)(H_A)
 >=[sqrt(C B_n)-(sqrt(C)-1)]^2/(A B_n)
 >=(C/A)(1-B_n^(-1/2))^2.                               (8.5)

For each fixed seed let delta=C/A-1>0 and

 epsilon=1-sqrt((1+delta/2)/(1+delta))>0.

Since B_n>=2n+1, every integer n>=1 satisfying
2n+1>=epsilon^(-2) has the uniform deficit

                W_(mn)(H_A)>=(1+delta/2)U_(mn)^ZJ.      (8.6)

This constant is seed-dependent; no common gap for all seeds is inferred.
For an input ray n=b*2^j-1 the ACTUAL horizon is

                     mn=(r+1)(b*2^j-1),

so b=1 gives6*2^j-6 or7*2^j-7. By accepted proof Section13 the exact
limit is Z_(mb)/(A Z_b), which by(8.1) is at least1+delta. No four-step
193/25 threshold or j25 specialization has been transferred to these words.

## 9. Boundary and deletion checks; exact remaining scope

The scalar argument handles empty left or right children with capacity1
and product1. At r=1 the saturated condition forces t=sqrt(2)-1 and
A=1+sqrt(2), consistent with C_1. It uses no asymptotic approximation.
Nonnegative, rather than strictly positive, entries of Lambda are fully
included: the factors remain positive but their differences may vanish.
The strict proper-prefix condition comes from the positive future weighted
tail, not from assuming every Lambda entry is positive.

As a concrete deletion check, global product balance by itself does NOT
give the scalar capacity theorem. At length2 set

 t_0=1/3, t_1=(-5+2sqrt(13))/3,
 A=(5+2sqrt(13))/3.

Then A*t_0*t_1=1. Also A>81/20>C_2: the first inequality follows from
sqrt(13)>143/40, whose squared comparison is20800>20449. But the first
singleton has K q=(7/3)(1/3)=7/9<1. This word is outside the hereditary
class and outside the positive-factor class. Thus none of the proof
permits dropping its proper-interval constraints and retaining only the
unit-quadratic terminal balance.

The hereditary scalar relaxation itself has basic maximizers, but no
lossless reduction of the ORIGINAL primitive cone to this relaxation is
proved or used. The source-inspired positive two-sequence array is an
additional algebraic restriction; signed factors, different triangular
ranks, factor sums, and nonzero PSD remainders require their own original
sign/flow/space proofs. The nonzero-remainder four-step lane belongs to
max4 and has not been duplicated here.

This completes the requested whole-class obstruction for the genuinely
enlarged free-word class at the draft level. It neither supplies an
improving primitive seed nor resolves the unrestricted exact-profile Goal.
No fact_submit is authorized by this allocation and none was called.
The original full theorem has not been verified. This mathematical
handoff is not an inspected worker-process exit or a resource renewal.

## 10. Literature scope and local glossary

The factorization idea is from Salah R. Chikhi, *Last-Iterate Performance
of Gradient Descent and Relaxed Proximal Point via s-Composability*,
arXiv:2609.13765v2, Section4, formulas(10)--(14),
https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13765v2. Its exact local primary text
downloads/2609.13765v2.txt, Section4 and proof, was reread this round.
The source proves a constant-step result. No source theorem about
variable steps, proximal objectives, or this capacity obstruction is
imported. Sections1--9 here supply the new joint proofs in the original
smooth model. Matlas searches for both the free-word factorization and
the contiguous-product extremal statement returned unrelated theorems;
none was used. The new interval argument is a finite algebraic reduction
of the audited factorization, not a separate external theorem assumption.

Local glossary: r is physical seed length; t_i=h_i-1 its positive excess;
s and A are seed mass and capacity; U_i,D_j are positive factors in(1.3);
mu is the auxiliary oriented array; Lambda=2mu^T is the original array;
p_k=U_(k-1)D_k; d_i is auxiliary outgoing minus incoming flow; P_k is
prefix capacity; R_k is prefix reciprocal product; u_k,v_k are normalized
weighted prefix and suffix sums; b_k=v_(k-1); H_I,K_I,q_I are interval
mass, capacity, product; F_I=log(K_I q_I); c_*=sqrt(2)-1; Omega,C_n are
the original primitive kernel and capacities; G,B_n,Z_b,lambda,W_n and
U_n^ZJ retain the accepted benchmark meanings. In the physical comparison
m=r+1, H_A is the actual substituted schedule, D_A its dominant mass,
C=C_r, delta=C/A-1 and epsilon is the explicit positive loss threshold
in Section8. Induction symbols a,c are child capacities, d a bridge
excess, and j its original physical index; in the interval-overlap test
a,b,c instead denote the three explicitly stated physical piece masses.

# C112: one bounded joint-variable primitive-seed pilot

Status: UNVERIFIED research derivation and numerical discovery plan. No formal
gate is allocated. The original target remains open. Accepted mathematical
inputs are 04219554e41d9750 (Sections 10--13), 1252b8823bb5a290
(interpolation orientation), and 4c92d3fc4eb6153e (conditional thresholds/lift).
No shared draft is used as a mathematical premise.

## Full original model and the exact reduction

For a physical length r, keep every h_i>=0, 0<=i<r, and every ordered
Lambda_ij>=0, 0<=i,j<=r, i!=j; put Lambda_ii=0. Define s=sum h_i,
A=1+s, e=ones(r+1), T_ii=1, T_it=h_t for t<i, and zero otherwise.
Write L=diag(Lambda e)-Lambda, omega=(-2h_0,...,-2h_(r-1),2s)^T,
D=diag(h_i(2-h_i),-s(s+2)), and M=D+(LT+T^T L^T)/2.
The original constraints are L^T e=omega and M PSD on all R^(r+1).

The accepted original identity is V_h+sum Lambda_ij Q_ij=<M,Gram(g)>.
Here Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2<=0 by
1252b8823bb5a290, x_i=x_0-sum_(t<i)h_t g_t, and
V_h=2sum h_i(f_i-f_r)+sum h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2-s(s+1)||g_r||^2.
In a direct coefficient audit the value coefficient is canceled by the flow;
the position terms are sym(L(T-I)); the gradient-difference terms are
sym(L)-diag(omega)/2. Addition of V_h's diagonal gives exactly M.
This is the full original coordinate certificate, not an actual-gradient
quotient. Every zero-step coordinate and every ordered multiplier survives.

Here is the joint algebra allowing a smaller factor. Since Le=0, the sum
of the flow residuals is zero: e^T(L^T e-omega)=0. Thus the first r flow
equations imply the terminal equation. Put H_i=sum_(t<i)h_t. Then
(Te)_i=1+H_i, including H_r=s, and under the flow equations

  e^T D e=-sum h_i^2-s^2,
  e^T L T e=omega^T T e=2s^2-2sum h_i H_i
             =s^2+sum h_i^2,

because 2sum h_i H_i=s^2-sum h_i^2. Consequently e^T M e=0.
For a PSD symmetric M this forces Me=0: for any vector z, positivity of
(e+t z)^T M(e+t z) for both signs of real t makes the linear coefficient
2 z^T Me vanish. Conversely, if the first r entries of Me vanish,
e^T M e=0 forces its last entry to vanish as well.

Let P denote the leading r-by-r principal block of M, let u=ones(r),
and let B be the (r+1)-by-r matrix formed by stacking I_r above -u^T.
For symmetric M with Me=0, its last column is (-Pu,u^T P u)^T,
so M=B P B^T. In particular M PSD iff P PSD: one implication is
principal restriction, and the other follows from this identity.

Every real PSD P has P=K K^T for a lower triangular real K with
nonnegative diagonal, including singular P. A proof by induction:
if P_00=0 then positivity of its two-coordinate restrictions forces
P_0j=0 for all j; put the first column of K equal to zero and factor
the remaining PSD principal block. If P_00>0, set K_00=sqrt(P_00)
and K_i0=P_i0/K_00. The Schur complement is PSD, since its quadratic
form is that of P on (-P_00^(-1)P_0,rest z,z); factor it inductively.
This argument imposes neither strict diagonal positivity nor a rank cap.

Hence the joint polynomial model is exactly

  (L^T e-omega)_i=0                      for i<r,
  (Me)_i=0                              for i<r,
  M_ij-(K K^T)_ij=0                     for 0<=i<=j<r,
  h_i>=0, Lambda_ij>=0, K_ii>=0.

It has r+r(r+1)+r(r+1)/2 variables and 2r+r(r+1)/2 equalities.
Full M=B K K^T B^T is read back from the original model; the dropped
terminal equations are explicitly reported. All retained expressions have
degree at most two jointly in h,Lambda,K. Finite numerical boxes below
are additional search restrictions, never asserted global bounds.

## Independent calibration with a nonzero remainder

Use r=1, let h vary in [3/4,5/4], and maximize h. Set a=Lambda_01,
b=Lambda_10. Flow gives b=a+2h. Direct multiplication then gives

  M_00=h(2-h)+a(1-h),
  M_01=-a(1-h/2)-h(1-h),
  M_11=a-h^2,
  (Me)_0=h(1-a/2).

Since h>0 the null equation forces a=2, b=2+2h, and
M=(2-h^2)[[1,-1],[-1,1]]. Thus the exact constrained maximum is
h=5/4, a=2, b=9/2, K=sqrt(7)/4. Its remainder is nonzero.
The exact start h=1,a=2,b=4,K=1 is feasible. This calibration exercises
the same joint polynomial construction, full readback, and monitored
InteriorPoint solver intended for the two physical lengths.

## Independently justified full-rank warm-start words

For either r=5 or r=6 take h_i=1 for every physical i. The following
explicit array, used only to justify/start the word, is feasible:

  Lambda_(i,i+1)=2(i+1)^2,
  Lambda_(i+1,i)=2(i+1)(i+2),  0<=i<r,
  all other ordered entries=0.

All entries are nonnegative. At each nonterminal node, out-in=-2;
at the terminal node it is 2r. With T the lower triangular all-ones
matrix, direct row multiplication gives (LT)_ii=2i(i+1) for i<r,
(LT)_rr=2r(r+1), (LT)_(i,i+1)=-2(i+1)^2, and every other off-diagonal
entry zero. Adding D and symmetrizing gives

  M=sum_(i=0)^(r-1)(i+1)^2 (e_i-e_(i+1))(e_i-e_(i+1))^T.

Here e_i are the original coordinate unit columns. This PSD matrix
has kernel precisely span(e), since zero quadratic form means all
successive coordinates agree. Its leading principal block is positive
definite. Thus the original h=(1,...,1) word has a genuinely feasible
full original certificate and a nonzero remainder of maximal possible
rank r. The optimization model will NOT retain the path support or
the unit-step restrictions: all original h and Lambda become free.

## Fixed choices before reservation

Use existing Wolfram Language only; no installation. One calibration,
at most one fixed-word CSDP warm start per r, and at most one FindMinimum
InteriorPoint run per r. The warm-start SDP maximizes the least eigenvalue
of the leading principal block, with all original equations, 0<=Lambda<=400,
and an auxiliary 0<=tau<=100. Use its unmodified point only if the original
flow/null residuals are <=1e-8, all signs are nonnegative, and the principal
block is positive definite. Otherwise use the exact array just displayed;
this predefined fallback involves no extra solver and no jitter.

For joint runs use the boxes 0<=h<=6, 0<=Lambda<=400, |K_ij|<=100,
and 0<=K_ii<=100. Maximize A=1+sum h. No factor diagonal is required
strictly positive. Method InteriorPoint, machine precision, AccuracyGoal 8,
PrecisionGoal 8, at most150 iterations and2000 monitored objective/constraint
evaluation callbacks. Each callback logs its index, iteration, elapsed time,
capacity and residual/sign summary; each iteration saves the full variable
vector. Callback cap and wall deadline terminate through Catch/Throw;
MaxIterations and TimeConstrained provide additional guards.

Each job's exact source and metadata must be persisted and atomically
reserved before evaluation. The ledger charges the ENTIRE job cap as a
conservative guard, never refunds it, and reports measured kernel time
separately. Calibration/warm-start/reconstruction caps are10s each; each
joint run cap is40s. Kernel work is guarded below that cap, with separate
export/local-persistence allowance included. The total six-job maximum is
120s. A prior-work700s GUARD, not claimed measured time, enforces the900s
global backstop. Raw output is saved before any result summary.

Stop discovery immediately if a candidate has A greater than the exact
accepted Gamma5=7977607/806000 or Gamma6=387153999/32240000 and passes
the full original numerical readback at tolerance1e-7. The convenient
targets are A>=99/10 and A>=1201/100. Numerical feasibility is not a seed
proof. Only the single reserved10s job may reconstruct/read back exactly.
No formal gate, extra run, restart, global bound or nonexistence claim.

## Methodological reference and retrieval record

Das Gupta, Van Parys, Ryu, *Branch-and-Bound Performance Estimation
Programming: A Unified Methodology for Constructing Optimal Optimization
Methods*, arXiv:2203.07305v5, Section3.1.2 and Algorithm1, are methodology
only. Their variables describe a different PEP. This pilot adapts the
fixed-method SDP start followed by joint Cholesky-variable local optimization.
No paper theorem is a correctness premise, and Stage3 branch-and-bound is
not implemented. The factor equivalence needed here is proved above.

The primary PDF was downloaded. Local PDF extraction tools were absent;
the browser PDF request failed. The primary HTML was downloaded and its
text extracted with the standard-library HTML parser; the specified sections
were read. Matlas returned unrelated truncated-total-least-squares and
condition-number results; none is imported.

Official documentation read before evaluation:
https://reference.wolfram.com/language/ref/FindMinimum.html
https://reference.wolfram.com/language/tutorial/ConstrainedOptimizationLocalNumerical.html
The latter states that EvaluationMonitor is invoked for objective and
equality/inequality evaluations and StepMonitor once per interior-point step.
The required Wolfram context tool was asked for documentation only; its
automatic Alpha queries returned 'No Results Found'. No such query evaluated
a word or supplies mathematical evidence.

## Length-five start update before its reservation

Latest guidance 0fdd0f1dbfd2b8f1 offers shared draft8ce8b65121586185 as a
warm-start lead. The following reproduces the needed identity rather than
using the draft as a mathematical premise. It replaces ONLY the previously
planned unit-word length-five start, before that job is reserved. Length six
keeps the unit-word start. No additional solve or screen is introduced.

Fix t=9/10, a=sqrt(2), b=(-a+sqrt(10+8a))/2, p=a+b, lambda=1+a,
X=t a,Y=t b,C=1+X+Y,delta=1/10,d=(a-1)C-delta. The identity
b^2+ab=2lambda gives pb=2lambda. Put c=(C+d)/(C-d), k=1+2c,
Delta=d(2C+d)-C^2=-delta(2a C-delta), tau=-Delta/(C-d), eta=2tau,
u=2(X+Y)(1+X)/Y, v=2(X+Y)C/Y. The actual start is

  h=(X,Y,1+d,X,Y),
  Lambda=
   [[0,2,0,0,0,0],
    [2(1+X),0,u,0,0,0],
    [0,v,0,0,0,2cd+eta],
    [0,0,2cX,0,2k,2cX],
    [0,0,2cY,2k(1+X),0,ku+2cY],
    [0,0,2c+eta,0,kv,0]].

Every entry is nonnegative. To justify signs without numerical screening,
7/5<a<3/2 and the polynomial b^2+ab-2lambda at 3-a is 7-5a<0,
so p>3, C>5/2. Consequently d>1/2,0<d<C and C-d>C/2. Thus
c,k,u,v,tau,eta are positive. Using v-u=2(C-1) and c(C-d)=C+d,
the six divergences of the displayed array are respectively
-2X,-2Y,-2(1+d),-2X,-2Y,2(2C+d-1). This is the original flow.

For completeness, direct multiplication of the original child T,L,D for
word(X,Y) and array [[0,2,0],[2(1+X),0,u],[0,v,0]] yields
M_child=w0(g0-g1)^2+w1(g1-g2)^2, with
w0=2-X^2=2(1-t)(1+t),
w1=((X+Y)/Y)[2-Y^2+(2-Y)X]=2(p/b)(1-t)(1+lambda t).
The diagonal is (w0,w0+w1,w1), off-diagonal entries -w0,-w1,0;
these are obtained from M_00=2-X^2,M_12=(v(Y-1)-u)/2,
M_22=-(X+Y)(X+Y+2)+v and the full flow/null identities.

Here is also the parent coefficient audit. Let P=2,Z=5 and
E=X Q_(3,P)+Y Q_(4,P)+Q_(Z,P)+d Q_(P,Z)+X Q_(3,Z)+Y Q_(4,Z).
Its value part is 2X f3+2Y f4+(d-C)fP+(2-C-d)fZ;
its gradient part is X(1-X)||g3||^2+Y(1-Y)||g4||^2+
[(C-d)/2-d^2]||gP||^2+(C+d)||gZ||^2/2.
All mixed products cancel by substituting the actual GD positions.
Subtracting the two child energies from the parent and using
c Delta=k d^2-C^2 gives

 V_parent=V_left+k V_right-2c E+c Delta(||gZ||^2-||gP||^2).

The displayed array embeds the two child arrays, adds2c E and adds
eta to both (P,Z),(Z,P). With z=gP-gZ,J=X(g3-gZ)+Y(g4-gZ), direct
substitution gives Q_(P,Z)+Q_(Z,P)=-d||z||^2-(C+d)<z,gZ>-<z,J>.
The full parent remainder is therefore

 M=w0||g0-g1||^2+w1||g1-g2||^2
     +k[w0||g3-g4||^2+w1||g4-g5||^2]
     +tau[(C-d)||z||^2-2<z,J>].

This identity includes every original gradient and mixed entry. To prove
PSD, write U=g3-g4,V=g4-g5, R=X^2/w0+(X+Y)^2/w1,
q=tau/(C-d), and sigma=k-qR. Then
R=(1-t)^(-1)t^2[1/(1+t)+lambda/(1+lambda t)]. The bracketed
expression multiplied by t^2 is increasing for t>=0 by differentiation,
so at t=9/10 it is at most lambda/2. Hence

 (C-d)^2 sigma
  =(3C+d)(C-d)-(2a C-delta)t^2[1/(1+t)+lambda/(1+lambda t)]
  >(3/2)C^2-a lambda C >(15/4)C-(7/2)C=C/4>0.

The exact full sum of squares is

 w0||g0-g1||^2+w1||g1-g2||^2+sigma(w0||U||^2+w1||V||^2)
 +q||(X+Y)sqrt(w0/w1)U-X sqrt(w1/w0)V||^2
 +tau(C-d)||z-J/(C-d)||^2.

Indeed R(w0||U||^2+w1||V||^2)-||XU+(X+Y)V||^2 is exactly the
fourth squared norm before its q factor. All displayed weights are
positive. Its kernel is precisely the common-gradient line, proving
positive definiteness of the leading principal block. This reproduces
the fixed start's original signs, flow, and full PSD jointly from the
accepted primitive identity. It makes no improving-seed claim about this
start or the shared one-dimensional family. The local solve frees every
h,Lambda and factor coordinate, with no relation to t enforced.

The calibration reserved job1 matched its exact endpoint with maximum
retained-equation residual1.1026235480215973e-11,10 iterations and30
monitored callbacks. The raw tool emitted Unset::norep and a graphics
message outside its exported kernel message list; both are preserved.
Subsequent source uses independent atomic symbols instead of indexed
expressions as decision variables to avoid unnecessary localization
messages; the polynomial equations and method are unchanged.

# C112 bounded joint-variable pilot: no credible seed

The ONE round authorized by TASK.md and guidance81b4e3cbbe96d65a is
complete. Neither local run produced a credible sufficient-capacity
primitive seed. The original exact-profile problem is OPEN. No fact_submit,
formal verification, installation, paid consult, native subagent, publication,
finalization, revocation, shutdown, or extra solver run occurred.

All new mathematical derivations are UNVERIFIED author drafts. Numerical
calibration and readbacks are evidence about this implementation, not a fact
graph acceptance. The accepted inputs remain04219554e41d9750,
1252b8823bb5a290, and4c92d3fc4eb6153e.

## Model and starts

The complete joint derivation is model_and_plan.md. It rederives the original
V_h+sum Lambda Q identity, proves the flow-implied e^T M e=0 identity,
and proves equivalence of the original full PSD requirement to first-r flow
and null equations plus the entire leading-block lower-Cholesky identity.
The lifting basis B=[I_r;-ones(r)^T] gives M=B K K^T B^T. Zero factor
diagonals and every possible PSD rank are allowed. No original gradient
coordinate or ordered multiplier is deleted, including at zero steps.

The search moves every original h and every ordered Lambda jointly with
all lower-factor variables. The only extra restrictions are the explicitly
registered finite search boxes: h in[0,6], Lambda in[0,400], and each lower
factor entry in[-100,100] with nonnegative diagonal. These are not global
bounds. No symmetry, support, unit-step, repeated-gradient, zero-remainder,
or positive-rank restriction is imposed on a joint run.

The r=1 calibration maximizes h in[3/4,5/4]. Its independently derived
exact endpoint is h=5/4,Lambda01=2,Lambda10=9/2,
M=(7/16)[[1,-1],[-1,1]],K=sqrt(7)/4. The numerical endpoint had
maximum retained-equation residual1.1026235480215973e-11 and matched
this reference within the registered tolerance. There were10 step callbacks
and30 evaluation callbacks. Raw evaluator warnings are preserved.

For length5 the actual start is the fixed t=9/10 interior point suggested
by the max2 shared lead8ce8b65121586185. Its full signs, flow, and positive
rank-five remainder are reproduced jointly in model_and_plan.md; the draft
lead is not used as a correctness premise. The physical start is approximately

  (1.2727922061357855,1.4411086432670979,2.4383481011316324,
   1.2727922061357855,1.4411086432670979),
  A=8.866149799937398.

The actual length6 start is six unit steps, with the explicitly justified
path-Laplacian remainder and capacity7. Its principal Cholesky factor is
lower bidiagonal, with diagonal1,...,6 and subdiagonal-1,...,-5.
All other factor entries are zero. These sparsities describe starts only.

Each of the two permitted fixed-word CSDP solves attempted to improve the
principal eigenvalue margin with all ordered multipliers available. Both
outputs failed the preregistered original sign/residual test: some multipliers
were negative at approximately8e-7 in magnitude. Both used the predeclared
analytic anchor, with no clipping, PSD jitter, new word, or extra solve.
The selected length5 principal spectrum was positive, with least value
0.13375177387703158; length6 had least value0.558844956465161.

Both runs used one Wolfram Language15.0.1 FindMinimum invocation with

same under that positive scaling; no equality was rescaled or weakened.

made. Current TASK remains the one-round allocation.

## What the bounded outcome supports

This pilot supplies no actual sufficient-capacity certificate and no seed
proof. The accepted physical lift at18*2^j-6 or21*2^j-7 is therefore not
triggered. No conclusion about the unrestricted cone, global optimality,
nonexistence, or the original asymptotic minimax problem follows.

The concrete issue exposed here is feasibility convergence of the local
joint factor model: both nonlinear runs exhausted their iteration allowance
while the full matrix equalities remained too inaccurate, and both warm SDPs
also had sign/residual failures. A justified future direction, only with a
new allocation, is to repair that interface before further discovery—for
example, test an exact parametrization of the linear-in-Lambda flow/null
equations with all degeneracies retained, followed by a fully metered PSD
factor optimization. This is a proposal, not an established lossless
parametrization or an inference that the threshold can be reached. No further
run, analysis of subthreshold families, or formal gate is launched here.

Methodological sources, used only for implementation:
https://arxiv.org/abs/2203.07305v5 (Section3.1.2 and Algorithm1),
https://reference.wolfram.com/language/ref/FindMinimum.html,
https://reference.wolfram.com/language/tutorial/ConstrainedOptimizationLocalNumerical.html,
and official RawJSON/GZIP/BaseEncode documentation for transport.

# All quadratic primitive tests and all single cuts still miss the capacity scale

Status: complete self-contained candidate, NOT verifier-accepted. No new
solver or fact_submit. Earlier ruler estimates are re-proved here, not used
as unverified premises. This concerns a necessary-test relaxation of
primitive validity; it does NOT prove that the constructed schedules are
primitive for general smooth convex functions.

## Statement

Put c_sc=13/5, a_sc=c_sc^(-17), bsc_j=a_sc c_sc^j, and

    Hsc_0=(),  Hsc_(j+1)=(Hsc_j,bsc_j,Hsc_j),
    ssc_m=sum Hsc_m=a_sc(c_sc^m-2^m)/(c_sc-2).

Consider TWO consecutive copies

    Gsc_m=(Hsc_m,Hsc_m),
    Lsc_m=2(2^m-1),   Dsc_m=1+2ssc_m.                (1)

For any finite nonnegative schedule h of sum S define the intrinsic
primitive energy on an actual smoothness-one GD trajectory by

    V_h=2sum_(i<n)h_i(f_i-f_n)
       +sum_(i<n)h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2
       -S(S+1)||g_n||^2.                             (2)

The following assertions hold for every m:

(a) EVERY single-cut primitive test holds strictly for Gsc_m:

    Dsc_m(h_j-1)_+
      <(1+sum_(i<j)h_i)(1+sum_(i>j)h_i)              (3)

    for each actual step j.

(b) V_(Gsc_m)>=0 on EVERY finite-dimensional convex quadratic with a
    minimizer and gradient Lipschitz constant at most1, from every initial
    point. This tests every curvature, and the actual chronological
    primitive energy, not only the final polynomial or curvature1.

(c) With rho_sil=log_2(1+sqrt(2)),

    Dsc_m/(Lsc_m+1)^(rho_sil) -> infinity.             (4)

Consequently the conjunction of ALL quadratic primitive-energy tests and
ALL single-cut primitive tests cannot imply the proposed silver-power
capacity bound, or a constant multiple of that bound. The remaining
simplex/projection geometry is essential to any proof by such testing.
No claim is made here that V_(Gsc_m)>=0 for arbitrary nonlinear functions.

## 1. Meaning and necessity of the cut tests

For a selected step b>1 put A=1+sum before it and B=1+sum after it;
the total capacity is D=A+B+b-1. Projection onto C=[-v,u], with
u>0, v=(b-1)u/B and initial point Au, has gradient u on the entire
prefix, lands at -Bv after the selected step, then has gradient -v
on the entire suffix and ends at -v. Its fixed legal function is

    F_C(x)=max_(z in C)(xz-z^2/2),  F_C'=P_C.

Every sample has f_i=x_i g_i-g_i^2/2. Expanding the squared-distance
telescope in (2) gives

    V_h=x_0^2-(x_n+Sg_n)^2
       =A^2u^2-D^2(b-1)^2u^2/B^2.

Hence primitive validity requires D(b-1)<=AB. For b<=1 its positive-
part version is automatic. This proves necessity of the tests in (3),
including all function values, independently of earlier findings.

## 2. All cuts in the two-copy ruler pass

Abbreviate c=c_sc, a=a_sc, s_j=ssc_j, b=bsc_j. For j>=17,

    s_j/b=[1-(2/c)^j]/(c-2)>=eta,  eta=13/8.          (5)

Indeed (2/c)^3<1/2 and (2/c)^2<4/5 imply (2/c)^17<1/40.
Every step b>1 has j>=18. A step b<=1 satisfies (3) strictly because
the right side is positive.

For a fixed level j<m, the first occurrence of b in Gsc_m is preceded
by exactly Hsc_j, and the last occurrence is followed by exactly Hsc_j.
The two-copy word is palindromic. Thus the prefix sum at any occurrence
lies between s_j and 2s_m-s_j-b. For fixed total step sum, the product
of the two cut masses is concave in the prefix sum, and the two endpoint
values agree. Its minimum is therefore

    (1+s_j)(1+2s_m-s_j-b).

Put s=s_j and L=2s_m. The margin over the cut left side is

    (1+s)(1+L-s-b)-(1+L)(b-1).

Its derivative in L is s+2-b>0 by (5). Since m>=j+1,
L>=2s_(j+1)=4s+2b. At this minimum L the margin is

    3s^2-3sb-2b^2+8s+2b+2.

The quadratic part is increasing for s>=eta b and is at least

    (3eta^2-3eta-2)b^2=(67/64)b^2>0.

Every margin is therefore strictly positive. This proves (a).

## 3. The exact quadratic energy and its chronological moment

Fix scalar curvature lambda in(0,1], initial point1, and
f(x)=lambda x^2/2. For ANY physical schedule h define

    P_i=product_(t<i)(1-lambda h_t),
    Q=|P_n|, tau=lambda sum h_i,
    R=lambda sum_(i<n) h_i P_i^2.

The quantity R includes every actual prefix product; order has not been
discarded. Direct substitution into (2), or the identity
1-Q^2=sum_i(2lambda h_i-lambda^2 h_i^2)P_i^2, gives

    V_h=1-[1+(1+lambda)tau+tau^2]Q^2-(1-lambda)R.     (6)

For the single word Hsc_j write Q_j,R_j,tau_j, and put
z_j=lambda bsc_j. The exact concatenation recurrences are

    Q_(j+1)=|1-z_j| Q_j^2,
    R_(j+1)=R_j+Q_j^2[(1-z_j)^2 R_j+z_j],
    tau_(j+1)=2tau_j+z_j.                             (7)

These formulas include the middle step and both copies in their actual
order. For two copies of Hsc_m the corresponding quantities are

    Q_double=Q_m^2,
    R_double=R_m(1+Q_m^2),
    tau_double=2tau_m.                                (8)

## 4. Any normalized-small-step word has nonnegative quadratic energy

Suppose every z_i=lambda h_i lies in[0,1]. During a chronological
prefix let tau,R,Q have their analogous partial meanings and set

    B_pot=R+(1+tau+tau^2)Q^2.

On adding a step z in[0,1], use R'=R+zQ^2, tau'=tau+z,
and (Q')^2=(1-z)^2Q^2. Exact expansion yields

    B_pot'-B_pot
      =-Q^2[tau^2 z(2-z)+tau z^2(3-2z)+z^3(1-z)]<=0.

Initially B_pot=1, so it remains at most1. Each partial product has
magnitude at least the final product, hence R>=tau Q^2. Equation (6)
can now be written

    V_h=1-B_pot+lambda(R-tau Q^2)>=0.                 (9)

This argument applies to any order and any physical h with lambda h<=1;
it does not require physical h<=1.

## 5. Controlling the moment before and at first activation

Let I be the first level for which t=z_I>1, and put J=I+1. Then
I>=18 and 1<t<=c. For all j>=17, (5) gives tau_j>=eta z_j.

First bound R_I. Put z=t/c in(1/c,1], so z is the central step of
Hsc_I. All normalized steps inside Hsc_(I-1) are at most z/c. If
q=Q_(I-1)^2, the squared-distance telescope there gives

    R_(I-1)<=(1-q)/(2-z/c).

Also q<=exp(-2tau_(I-1))<=exp(-2eta z), since those factors are in[0,1].
Set C=1/(2-z/c). Substituting into (7) and dropping a nonpositive term,

    R_I<=C+C z^2(1-1/c)q
        <=[c/(2c-1)] [1+(1-1/c)/(eta^2 exp(2))]
        <(13/21)[1+512/15379]<2/3.                   (10)

Here z^2 exp(-2eta z)<=1/(eta^2 exp(2)) follows by differentiating,
and exp(2)>7 follows from its power series. The final rational comparison
uses 13*512<15379.

The complete product on Hsc_I satisfies Q_I<=exp(-eta t).
Its first activated extension therefore has

    z_J Q_J<=c t(t-1)exp(-2eta t)
             <=3c/(16eta^3)=1248/10985<k_0:=1/8.     (11)

Use exp(2eta t)>(2eta t)^3/6 and (t-1)/t^2<=1/4 for this bound.
Meanwhile (7) and (10) give

    R_J<2/3+exp(-2eta t)[(2/3)(t-1)^2+t]<35/48.      (12)

To check the second inequality, put B(t)=(2/3)(t-1)^2+t. The polynomial
2B-B'=(4t^2-6t+5)/3 is positive, so B(t)exp(-2eta t) decreases
for t>=1, as 2eta>2. Its value at1 is exp(-13/4)<1/16.
The last bound follows from exp(3)>1+3+9/2+27/6+81/24>16.

## 6. Uniform moment control at every later level

For j>=J put w_j=z_j Q_j. Here z_j>=c>2. Equation (7) implies

    w_(j+1)=c(1-1/z_j)w_j^2<=c w_j^2.

With theta_0=c k_0=13/40, (11) therefore gives

    w_j<=k_0 theta_0^(j-J),
    sum_(j>=J) w_j^2<=k_0^2/(1-theta_0^2)=25/1431.  (13)

The moment recurrence also gives

    R_(j+1)-R_j<=w_j^2(R_j+1/z_j)
                         <=w_j^2(R_j+1/c).

Thus U_j=R_j+1/c obeys U_(j+1)<=(1+w_j^2)U_j. By (12)--(13),

    R_j+1/c
      <[35/48+5/13]/[1-25/1431]
      <3/4+5/13,

so R_j<3/4. We used product(1+w_j^2)<=exp(sum w_j^2)
<=1/[1-sum w_j^2]. The final exact comparison is
695*1431=994545<995448=59*12*1406.

Also tau_j<=z_j/(c-2)=5z_j/3 and Q_j^2<=k_0^2/z_j^2. Consequently

    [1+(1+lambda)tau_j+tau_j^2]Q_j^2
      <=(1/64)[1/c^2+10/(3c)+25/9]<1/8.             (14)

For the last simple inequality, the bracket is less than
1+4/3+3=16/3<8. This bound is uniform in lambda, its activation
phase t, and the horizon.

## 7. Every quadratic passes for the two-copy schedule

If m<=I, every normalized step in Gsc_m is at most1, so (9) applies.
If m>=J, (13)--(14) give Q_m^2<=1/(64c^2)<1/256 and R_m<3/4.
Using (8),

    R_double < (3/4)(1+1/256)=771/1024.

Further,

    [1+(1+lambda)tau_double+tau_double^2]Q_double^2
      <=4[1+(1+lambda)tau_m+tau_m^2]Q_m^4
      <(1/2)Q_m^2<1/512.

Equation (6) therefore yields the explicit positive margin

    V_(Gsc_m)>1-771/1024-1/512=251/1024>0             (15)

for a unit scalar initial point once m>=J. Curvature lambda=0 gives
energy0 and is harmless. Before activation (9) supplies nonnegativity.

For an arbitrary finite-dimensional convex quadratic with a minimizer,
translate that minimizer to zero, subtract the minimum value, and
orthogonally diagonalize its positive semidefinite Hessian. Smoothness
at most1 places every eigenvalue in[0,1]. The GD trajectory and (2)
separate across these eigendirections, with each scalar energy multiplied
by the squared initial coordinate. The proved scalar nonnegativity
therefore proves (b), including singular Hessians and arbitrary starts.

## 8. Capacity comparison and what remains open

From (1),

    Dsc_m ~ [2a_sc/(c_sc-2)] c_sc^m,
    Lsc_m+1=2^(m+1)-1.

Since c_sc=13/5>1+sqrt(2), dividing by
(Lsc_m+1)^(rho_sil) proves (4). Thus this family passes the ENTIRE
quadratic primitive-energy class and every one-cut projection test while
its capacity exceeds any fixed multiple of the proposed silver-power
bound for all sufficiently large m.

This is a concrete failure of that necessary-test closure, not a
counterexample to the primitive-capacity conjecture. No universal
primitive certificate for Gsc_m is supplied. If these schedules fail
primitivity, their failure must use geometry beyond all quadratics and
the displayed single-cut functions. The strict simplex norm game is an
appropriate remaining interface because it retains that full geometry;
an equivalent formulation alone does not settle its capacity bound.

# Small modal radius does not control unrestricted common metrics

Status: complete UNVERIFIED construction and scope audit. No verifier or solver
call. This does not claim the original exact-profile target. The interpolation
and terminal conventions are those of accepted fact 1252b8823bb5a290.

## 1. An actual legal counterexample to a radius-only metric extension

Take the schedule (10,1). Its retained queried prefix is the single step10,
so its retained mass is S=10, with final free step b=1 in [0,2]. Use three
positive real modal parameters

        z_1=1/100, z_2=2/100, z_3=3/100.

Every S z_j is at most3/10<1/3. Let

        alpha=(1128,-2303,1176).

Direct integer arithmetic gives

 sum alpha_j=1,  sum alpha_j z_j=1/2,
                         sum alpha_j z_j^2=1/4.        (1)

Define an injective real linear map A:R^3->R^4 by

 A(v_1,v_2,v_3)=(alpha_1 v_1+alpha_2 v_2+alpha_3 v_3,
                                  v_1,v_2,v_3)/2.     (2)

Its metric is the strictly positive definite matrix
A^T A=(alpha alpha^T+I_3)/4; it includes nonzero initial correlations.
Also ||A(1,1,1)||=1 exactly. Define, on R^4,

 F(X)=X_0^2/4+X_1^2/200+X_2^2/100+3X_3^2/200.          (3)

This function is convex and differentiable, has gradient Lipschitz
constant1/2<=1, and has its unique minimizer at0. Thus it is admissible
for normalized smoothness1. Start at x_0=(1,1,1,1)/2. Its gradient is
g_0=(1/2,1/100,2/100,3/100)/2. The actual first step gives

 x_1=(-4,9/10,8/10,7/10)/2,
 g_1=(-2,9/1000,16/1000,21/1000)/2.                     (4)

For P_0(z)=1 and P_1(z)=1-10z, identities (1) show exactly that

 x_i=A(P_i(z_1),P_i(z_2),P_i(z_3)),
 g_i=A(z_1 P_i(z_1),z_2 P_i(z_2),z_3 P_i(z_3)), i=0,1. (5)

The last gradient g_2 is unrestricted by the modal ansatz. The actual
last step x_2=x_1-g_1 has first coordinate -1, so

                        2F(x_2) > 1/2.                (6)

Every prefix/optimum interpolation inequality holds, since these are
actual jets of the explicit global function (3). The initial distance
is exactly one and the minimizer is genuinely0. Thus this is an actual
legal polynomial-image trajectory, with a FULL SPD common metric,
three small positive REAL modes, and an allowed final step.

In particular it refutes the proposed upgrade that arbitrary common
metrics at S|z|<=1/3 must obey the normal-mixture Huber cap
2F(x_1-b g_1)<=1/[2S+1+b(2-b)]. That right side is1/22 at b=1, whereas
(6) is larger than1/2. The earlier normal and bounded-coupling theorems
did not assert such an upgrade, and this example does not contradict
them. It also does not contradict the specific two-complex-mode
near-critical obstruction in finding cebf601250c82dc2: the spectra,
chronology, and metric assumptions are different.

## 2. Why the scope changes when the number of modes grows

This is a warning about extrapolation, not a proposed arbitrary-Gram
research program. For a positive schedule of length n>=1, select ANY
n+1 distinct positive real nodes theta_j, even inside an arbitrarily
small interval. Let P_i(z)=product_(t<i)(1-h_t z). The polynomials

                  1, zP_0(z), ..., zP_(n-1)(z)          (7)

have successive degrees0,1,...,n and nonzero leading coefficients.
They form a basis of polynomials of degree at most n. Their evaluations
at the n+1 distinct nodes are an invertible square matrix: a linear
combination vanishing at all nodes would be a polynomial of degree at
most n with n+1 roots, and hence zero; its basis coefficients must vanish.
This is the same polynomial-degree argument used in the strict-primal
proof of accepted fact1252b8823bb5a290.

Given ANY actual normalized legal prefix vectors x_0,g_0,...,g_(n-1),
define a real linear map A on the basis evaluation columns in (7) by

 A(1,...,1)=x_0,
 A((theta_j P_i(theta_j))_j)=g_i.

Invertibility defines A uniquely. The polynomial identity
P_i(z)=1-sum_(t<i)h_t zP_t(z) then gives

 A((P_i(theta_j))_j)=x_0-sum_(t<i)h_t g_t=x_i

for all0<=i<=n, including the unqueried final position. Thus every legal
prefix has this modal representation at arbitrarily small positive nodes
when the full PSD metric A^T A is unrestricted. The prefix values are
unchanged, the initial norm is unchanged, and the terminal gradient stays
free, exactly as required in the reduced formulation of fact1252.

Zero steps cause identical queried polynomials and identical actual
jets at those positions. Retain one representative of each distinct
queried polynomial. Their degrees are distinct. They can be extended
to a basis of all polynomials of degree at most n, and the unused basis
vectors can be assigned arbitrary images, giving the same representation
with n+1 nodes. This also directly covers a last queried zero step,
without assuming an unqueried endpoint gradient identity.

Consequently the unrestricted full-metric, n+1-node modal formulation
with every joint interpolation constraint and a free terminal gradient
is exactly as strong as the full original fixed-schedule problem. It
does not furnish a new lower bound by itself. A radius argument alone
cannot make this enlarged formulation simpler. A scalable proof must
identify additional genuine structure, or solve the full interpolation
problem it encodes.

## 3. Implication for the next model

The completed two-mode bound controls a fixed four-dimensional temporal
polynomial span, not merely the ambient Euclidean dimension or spectral
radius. Changing only the metric within that precise span is now closed
by the candidate proof. A new direction must change the temporal span or
the trajectory mechanism and still prove all joint legality and the
correct benchmark payoff. Neither the finite example (6) nor the exact
representation (7) is an asymptotic answer to the original problem.
